## Slice queue — Wave 1 (from the roadmap, verbatim titles)
- S-153 Merge: merge commit — PR title/body as default message, editable, closing keywords executed on landing.
- S-154 Merge: squash — one commit, combined message, author preserved, closings executed.
- S-155 Merge: rebase — head commits replayed onto base; refused loudly when conflicts would result.
- S-156 Conflict surfacing — mergeability computed off-path; conflicts shown before merging, never discovered by merging.
- S-157 Checks panel — external status/check results listed on the PR with links; states said in text+icon, never colour-only.
- S-158 Auto-merge — merge when requirements pass; cancellation and full audit.
- S-159 Branch housekeeping — delete head branch on merge (opt-in), restore branch action, fork sync hint.
- S-160 Revert — one-click PR that reverts a merged PR, linked both ways.
- S-161 PR search — Part X qualifier language extended (is:pr, review:, status:).
- S-162 PR templates — single and per-directory templates; template picker on creation.
- S-169 Protection rule model — rules attach to branch-name patterns per repo; evaluation order is documented and deterministic. (team-07 — BANKED 2026-11-06, keeper)
- S-168 Part VII gate — review battery: full lifecycle across roles, forks, drafts, and all three merge methods, counted green. (S-163..S-167 are assigned to teams 01..05 above; S-161..S-168 banked 2026-11-06 (keeper) — PART VII GATE PASSED at S-168. Banking now waits on S-171 (team-09, assigned, not yet built).)
- S-170 Require pull request before merging — direct pushes to a protected branch refused at receive-pack with a human-readable reason. (team-08 — BANKED 2026-11-06, keeper)
- S-174 Required status checks — named checks must be green; branch must be up-to-date toggle honoured. (team-02, after S-164 banked)

## Banking (per slice, strictly in roadmap order)
**NEW BANKING LAW — Volmarr's direct order, 2026-11-22 (~07:52 EST).** The old banking regime is abolished and its crews are fired. The old rule required the FULL test suite green in the team worktree before any slice could bank; the suite has grown to 5,461 tests and a single run takes ~6.1 hours wall on this shared laptop (S-336's closure records "Ran 5461 in 21922.419s"), which had slowed banking to ~1 slice per day. Volmarr's ruling: remove the idea of using 6-hour tests — "that is stupid."
1. A slice banks when (a) its own battery is green and (b) its neighbour suites are green. NO full-suite run gates banking. Ever.
2. The full suite runs ONCE per wave of 10 banked slices (at banked counts 340, 350, ...), in the background, non-blocking. If it surfaces a regression, fixing it becomes the immediate next work (bug law: never leave a discovered bug unfixed) — fix forward; the gate is never re-imposed.
3. The suite itself is being sped up — sharded by test module across worker processes, disk-backed TMPDIR per shard; findings and the new wall time are recorded in this plan under "Suite speed".
5. THE 2-HOUR FIRING RULE — Volmarr, 2026-11-22 07:56 EST: if the crew goes 2 hours without banking a slice, the crew is fired and a replacement is mustered. The clock measures from the later of muster time and the last banked slice; every banking resets it. The samverk-swift-keeper tracks the stamps in this plan and enforces the rule. Last banking: S-337 at 2026-11-22 ~07:59 EST (d6854ae) — clock resets at each "Map: S-NNN banked" commit.
4. Mechanics (unchanged): build as a complete vertical slice (inspect → build → test → repair → continue) → the coordinator merges the team branch into the main checkout on purplelongship → git fetch + remote-diff before any push → push explicit refspec HEAD:refs/heads/main, never force → verify with git ls-remote → update PROJECT_MEMORY_MAP.md and this plan in the same commit or the next map commit. One slice per commit, subject format "S-NNN: <title>" with the house-style body (what was built, invariants, test counts read back from log files). Never leave a discovered bug unfixed: fix it in-slice or record it as the immediate next work with the discovery written down. README.md is NEVER touched. NO Docker.
- S-175 Require signed commits — pushes and merges carrying unsigned commits refused (verification itself lands in Part XV; the rule shape lands here). (team-03 — BANKED 2026-11-06, keeper)
- S-176 Require linear history — merge commits refused on the branch; squash/rebase merges remain available. (team-04 — BANKED 2026-11-06, keeper)
- S-177 Block force pushes and branch deletion — on protected patterns, for everyone, admins included unless the S-179 bypass is used. (team-05 — BANKED 2026-11-06, keeper)

- S-178 Restrict who can push — named users/teams only may push matching branches directly (where direct push is allowed at all). (team-06 — BANKED 2026-11-06, keeper)

- S-179 Bypass list — named bypassers per rule; every bypass is audit-logged and shown on the rule (no silent doors). (team-07 — BANKED 2026-11-06, keeper)
- S-189 Team repository permissions — the role ladder read / triage / write / maintain / admin, applied per repo per team. (team-07 — BANKED 2026-11-07, keeper)
- S-180 Enforce for admins — the toggle GitHub users know; default ON for Samverk instances (sovereignty means the owner's rules bind the owner). (team-08 — BANKED 2026-11-06, keeper)
- S-190 Permission resolution engine — one function: user × repo → role, considering ownership, teams (with nesting), collaborator grants, and visibility; the full matrix is a test fixture. (team-08, after S-180 banked)
- S-171 Required approving reviews — count configurable; self-approval never counts; last-pusher approval rule available. (team-09 — BANKED 2026-11-06, keeper)
- S-181 Rulesets — one ruleset targets many repos/branches/tags; layered rules resolve most-restrictive-wins; the evaluation is a tested matrix. (team-09 — BANKED 2026-11-06, keeper)
- S-191 Outside collaborators — per-repo grants to non-members, listed in one auditable place, revocable in one click. (team-09, after S-181 banked)
- S-192 Default repository permission — org-wide base role for members, settable, shown in effect on the matrix page. (team-10 — BANKED 2026-11-07, keeper)
- S-202 Billing-shaped hole, honestly empty — Samverk is free software on your own machines; no seat/billing concepts exist, and the docs say why. (team-10 — BANKED 2026-11-07, keeper)
- S-172 Dismiss stale reviews — new pushes dismiss approvals per S-149, wired into the rules engine. (team-10 — BANKED 2026-11-06, keeper)
- S-182 Tag protection — tag name patterns protected from deletion and force-move; release tags get the strongest default. (team-10 — BANKED 2026-11-06, keeper)
- S-192 Default repository permission — org-wide base role for members, settable, shown in effect on the matrix page. (team-10, after S-182 banked)

- S-185 Organizations — create, profile (name, bio, avatar with alt text, website), settings home where every control is findable. (team-03, after S-175 banked)
- S-186 Org membership — owners and members; the last owner cannot leave or be removed (invariant proven in test). (team-04 — BANKED 2026-11-07, keeper)
- S-187 Teams — create, describe, privacy (visible/secret), team pages listing members and repos. (team-05 — BANKED 2026-11-07, keeper)
- S-197 Internal visibility — repos visible to all org/instance members, distinct from public and private, enforced by S-190. (team-05 — BANKED 2026-11-07, keeper)
- S-207 Code indexing — on push, changed blobs extracted to the index within the size/language budgets; LFS pointers indexed as pointers, honestly. (team-05 — BANKED 2026-11-07, keeper)
- S-188 Nested teams — parent/child teams with permission inheritance documented and visualised before saving. (team-06 — BANKED 2026-11-07, keeper)
- S-198 Org audit log — membership, permission, and repo events for the org, readable by owners (storage is S-007's log). (team-06, after S-188 banked)
- S-196 Org repositories — creation policies (who may create, allowed visibilities), org repo list with filters. (team-04 — BANKED 2026-11-07, keeper)

- S-183 Rule insights — a page answering why was this push/merge refused, from the audit trail, in plain words. (team-01 — BANKED 2026-11-06, keeper)
- S-184 Part VIII gate — rules battery: every rule × every role × both enforcement points, counted green. (team-02 — BANKED 2026-11-07, keeper)
- S-194 Member removal cascade — what happens to assignments, reviews, and ownerships on removal is computed and shown before confirming. (team-02, after S-184 banked)
- S-193 Invitations — invite by username or email, expiring, rescindable; acceptance creates membership and audit events on both sides. (team-01 — BANKED 2026-11-07, keeper)
- S-203 Instance-wide groups seam — site-level groups share the team machinery (used by admin policy in Part XVIII). (team-01, after S-193 banked)

- S-185 Organizations — create, profile (name, bio, avatar with alt text, website), settings home where every control is findable. (team-03 — BANKED 2026-11-07, keeper)
- S-195 Team mentions — @org/team notifies the team (Part XI), usable in issues, PRs, and review requests. (team-03, after S-185 banked)

- S-190 Permission resolution engine — one function: user × repo → role, considering ownership, teams (with nesting), collaborator grants, and visibility; the full matrix is a test fixture. (team-08 — BANKED 2026-11-07, keeper)
- S-200 Role change guardrails — demoting the last admin of a repo is refused with the reason stated. (team-08, after S-190 banked)

- S-191 Outside collaborators — per-repo grants to non-members, listed in one auditable place, revocable in one click. (team-09 — BANKED 2026-11-07, keeper)
- S-201 Permission preview — “view as” for admins: see the instance exactly as a chosen member sees it (the findability and safety tool). (team-09 — BANKED 2026-11-07, keeper)
- S-206 Issue/PR search backend — the qualifier language of S-135/S-161 executed over FTS + structured fields. (team-04 — BANKED 2026-11-07, keeper)

- S-199 Org deletion — owner-only, sudo mode, inventory + grace as S-043; repos must be transferred or deleted explicitly first. (team-07 — BANKED 2026-11-07, keeper)

- S-209 Permission-filtered results — search never reveals a title, snippet, or count from a repo the searcher cannot read; leakage battery included. (team-07 — BANKED 2026-11-07, keeper)

## Suite speed
- Diagnosis (2026-11-22, full record in the main checkout's hidden_files/suite-speed-findings.md): (a) the suite ran as ONE serial unittest-discover process - 339 modules / 5,478 tests on effectively one core; (b) contention inflated it ~3-10x - the dominant consumer on this box is NOT Samverk (a foreign graph_builder.py cycles at 850-1,280% CPU); mid-run observation of S-336: 21,922s wall vs ~1h44 CPU. True serial work is only ~3,200-3,600s CPU (~1 hour). (c) Slowest cells: the Part-gate modules (test_part_x_gate 138.6s/35, test_part_xiv_gate 42.1s/26, test_part_vii_gate 22.5s/15), test_global_search 53.6s, test_custom_patterns 32.3s.
- The fix: tools/run_suite_sharded.py (pure stdlib, banked with this record) shards modules by file size across worker processes, per-shard disk TMPDIR, aggregated verdict; --serial-tail exists for any future colliders (none found: 324/339 modules use mkdtemp/TemporaryDirectory). First benchmark (8 workers, worst-case load ~24-34): externally killed at 57.6 min wall with ~47% executed, ZERO failures/errors, no collisions; a fully-detached rerun (label wave337b, markers hidden_files/bench-wave337b-done.txt) is in flight and its final Ran/wall lands here when it completes. On a quiet box the runner is expected at ~8-15 min. The wave-340 full-suite run launches on the current tree from this runner once the rerun proves it end-to-end.
